## 0. How Codex / Any AI Agent MUST Use This File

**Read every coding session, before editing:** `PRD.MD` → `AGENTS.md` → `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` → **`todo.md`** → relevant accepted ADRs → actual Git state. If repo facts contradict this tracker, **investigate and correct the tracker with evidence**, never fake repository state.

1. Locate an issue/task by its stable ID (`M0-01` … `M6-08`, or `ISS-0001` …). Do not introduce duplicate work items with new IDs.
2. Check task dependencies and any required **human-accepted ADR**; do not implement security-critical semantics solely from draft proposals.
3. Claim **one bounded task**: change `NOT_STARTED` → `IN_PROGRESS`, fill `Owner`, `Last updated` and the active session log. Multiple agents may own separate tasks but must coordinate files and interfaces.
4. Record code paths, PRD/AC IDs, expected failure cases, tests to run, and security boundary before making changes; put detailed design in ADR/PR rather than bloating this tracker.
5. If blocked, use `BLOCKED`, record the **specific unblock condition** in Blocker Register, and stop unauthorized activity; never convert an unapproved action into a background assumption.
6. After code changes, run appropriate actual checks, preserve negative/security test output, and record command results with commit/PR/file paths in the **Evidence Log**. Do not mark a task DONE when checks have not run or an approval is missing.
7. Use `IN_REVIEW` for completed work awaiting validation/approval. Mark `DONE` only when the architecture task's concrete **Done when** statement is supported by accessible evidence and required review is complete.
8. At the end of **every session**, update the task row(s), milestone summary, Current Focus, Open Issues/Blockers and Session Journal with exact facts and next task. Never claim unseen results.
9. Preserve historical log entries and immutable evidence. If work regresses, reopen the task with an explicit explanation; do **not** erase past DONE/evidence events.
10. Do not let this document become a security authorization token. Task status `DONE` or a written approval note in Markdown **does not** authorize running tests, approving contracts, sending data to an LLM, or changing production policy.

**Status vocabulary — synchronized with `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §7:**

| Status | Meaning | Entry / exit rule |
|---|---|---|
| `NOT_STARTED` | Planned, no verified task execution yet | Default on creation; can be selected when prerequisites are satisfied |
| `IN_PROGRESS` | Someone is actively working on it | Must have owner, scope and journal entry |
| `BLOCKED` | Stopped by a concrete unmet prerequisite or decision | Blocker ID, unblock condition and next responsible person required |
| `IN_REVIEW` | Work exists, review/tests/evidence gate pending | Include artifact paths, tests, reviewer, unresolved checks |
| `DONE` | Exact work-item DoD and required reviews genuinely met | Verified evidence log + dates + PRD/AC trace required |
| `DEFERRED` | Intentionally postponed by owner | Record rationale, approver and revisit trigger; not counted as done |

**Transitions:** `NOT_STARTED → IN_PROGRESS → IN_REVIEW → DONE`; `IN_PROGRESS/IN_REVIEW → BLOCKED`; `BLOCKED → IN_PROGRESS` after documented resolution; `DONE → IN_PROGRESS` only with explicit reopening event; `NOT_STARTED → DEFERRED` when authorized. Never jump directly from `NOT_STARTED` to `DONE` without logging executed work and verification.

```text
Inspect git status and filesystem; don't assume any feature exists.
Pick M0-01, check prerequisites, mark IN_PROGRESS with owner/date.
Perform ONLY its acceptance scope; record evidence and update todo.md.
Show tests performed, remaining decisions and the next unblocked work item.
Do not claim DONE without proof or self-approve an ADR.
```

## 4. Approval and Architecture Decision Register

**This table tracks the decision workflow; it does not constitute authenticated approval.** An ADR becomes authoritative only after an actual owner/security reviewer accepts its exact contents via the established review process.

| ADR ID | Related questions | Decision area | Status | Reviewer / proof | Blocks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ADR-0001` | OQ-02 | Canonical contract/authorization DSL, rule syntax, DENY semantics | `ACCEPTED — specified design choices` | Direct developer decision during M1-01; exact EV-0003 ADR/example hashes verified, EV-0013; M1-02 JSON Schema/stdlib and conservative-overlap clarification, EV-0014 | G-01 DSL choices resolved; other approval/runner/transport/tooling gates unchanged |
| `ADR-0002` | OQ-03, OQ-06 | Authorized target, FastAPI fixtures, runner, 404 profile, redirect controls | `PROPOSED — changes requested; revised for review` | Developer; [v0.2 draft](docs/adr/0002-fixture-runner.md), EV-0007; v0.1 not approved | Runner/test execution trust after M0-04 |
| `ADR-0003` | OQ-04, OQ-14 | Trusted human review, hash-bound approval, audit and immutability | `PROPOSED — scoped storage direction selected; broader review pending` | Developer; [v0.2 draft](docs/adr/0003-human-approval.md), EV-0034; EV-0006 historical. Not full ADR acceptance or human mechanism decision | Real binding contract approval stays disabled; M0-05 IN_REVIEW |
| `ADR-0004` | PRD §8.3 | Stack/workspace packaging and compatibility | `PROPOSED — awaiting review` | Developer; [v0.1 draft](docs/adr/0004-repository-and-stack.md), EV-0008; no acceptance | Tooling baseline; bootstrap/matrix evidence remains future M0-07; no CI policy activation |
| `ADR-0005` | OQ-05 | MCP / Codex trust and tool boundaries | `NOT_STARTED` | — | Codex integration trust |
| `ADR-0006` | OQ-10 | Provider consent, egress and adapter interface | `NOT_STARTED` | — | External model usage |
| `ADR-0007` | OQ-08, OQ-13 | Hosted control plane and private runner trust | `NOT_STARTED` | — | M5 hosted runner |
| `ADR-0008` | OQ-11 | Webhooks, CI statuses, optional merge gate | `NOT_STARTED` | — | Merge enforcement |
| `ADR-0009` | OQ-12 | Benchmark methodology and acceptable claims | `NOT_STARTED` | — | Competitive evidence claims |

**When proposing ADRs:** replace a `NOT_STARTED` status with a factual workflow note such as `PROPOSED — awaiting review`; record the reviewer and artifact. Never write `ACCEPTED` on behalf of the coding agent.

**Historical M0-01 reconciliation:** At inventory time no ADR file or docs/DECISIONS.md existed; all ADR rows were NOT_STARTED and none accepted. M0-03/04/05 later added ADR-0001/0002/0003 as PROPOSED. The other six ADR rows remain unchanged and files absent. Schema §11 additionally proposes ADR-0010 for hosted RLS, but no file or new canonical task exists. The inventory §7 remains historical evidence; it is not rewritten to imply approval.

**M0-06 current reconciliation:** ADR-0004 now exists as PROPOSED; the remaining five planned ADR files are absent. The earlier reconciliation describes its historical snapshot; no acceptance is inferred.

### 4.1 Scoped design-direction acceptance — 2026-10-10

**Decision source:** Direct developer/repository-owner response to the EV-0010 review request. Before recording, DATA_MODEL v0.1, API_SPEC v0.1 and TEST_PLAN v0.2 hashes matched the reply and EV-0010. This is genuine scoped documentation/design acceptance, not a product approval event or final approval of all content. The owner said: “I grant SCOPED ACCEPTANCE ... subject to the conditions below” and “This is acceptance of specific design directions, not final approval of all document content.”

| Accepted direction / reviewed artifact | Conditions and exact remaining boundary |
|---|---|
| DATA_MODEL canonical domain/wire reference; schema.md historical/reference | Preserve original schema documentation, clearly prevent competing authoritative definitions. Historical notice + current reference relationship checked in EV-0011; no schema deletion or migration. |
| Typed opaque identifiers / DATA_MODEL and API_SPEC v0.1 | Consistent database/API/CLI/MCP/evidence names; IDs never prove authorization; current org/project checks; explicit validation/serialization/backward-compatibility tests. Approach accepted, not implementation or every DTO field. |
| One-job/one-run per verification attempt / DATA_MODEL and API_SPEC v0.1 | Trace every admitted attempt, preserve version/scope/fixture/time/verdict/evidence, no retry overwrite, define idempotency/cancel/timeout/blocked/infra records, distinguish initially rejected submissions from performed verification. Concrete recovery/lease mechanisms remain open. |
| TEST_PLAN v0.2 §9 provisional baseline | Assess ten named security/design areas as COMPLETE/PARTIAL/MISSING/NOT YET APPLICABLE based on planned cases; no fabricated completed coverage/results. v0.3 §10 records bounded design assessment, all runtime NOT RUN. |

**Explicit exclusions:** No Security Contract approval, target/external security test grant, external AI provider/egress, privileged operation, production deployment, M0-02 sign-off or previously unresolved ADR acceptance. No certification that database/API/tests are implemented or validated. M0-08/09 remain IN_REVIEW; no dependent implementation task closed. New clarifications are requested follow-up, not implied full-document approval. Do not request approval again for unchanged directions accepted above; remaining gates are their existing security/tooling mechanisms and actual review/implementation evidence.

- **Answerable question templates (synthetic examples only):** For admin read scope: 'May an administrator read orders outside its primary store? Specify same-store only or the explicit additional scope.' For unresolved ownership: 'Which declared fact links this seller to an order: order owner, store ownership mapped to tenant, or tenant membership? Specify delegated/shared/multi-store cases.' For conflict: 'Rules R-ALLOW and R-DENY can both match an actor with seller and admin roles for this order/read case. Which behavior should the revised draft express, and what declared conditions separate the rules?' For malformed JSON: 'Resubmit a complete contract schema 1.0 JSON object with declared roles, resources, action and references; the rejected payload is not a usable contract.' Concrete rule refs must resolve; do not echo raw model text or sensitive values. These are product clarification examples, not current human approvals or questions asking the owner to settle a fictitious policy.
- **Bounds/state:** Proposed max 16 questions/case and 512 UTF-8 bytes per fixed-template question, existing bounded JSON input/depth/collection limits, bounded validated source/rule IDs; malformed/oversized/unknown diagnostics fail safely. Correlate the original source/version and rejected-content reference separately from approved contract hashes. Human answers are data for a new draft/revalidation only, never APPROVED, never auto-resolve an existing conflict; stale/mismatched references cannot update a case or history. This prototype implements no trusted answer/approval endpoint and freezes no public DTO.
- **Exact planned checks:** Valid unambiguous control produces no fabricated question/approval. Explicit admin/ownership overlap and different-role coexistence yield NEEDS_REVIEW with deterministic rule refs and a specific scope/condition question; undefined tenant/store/owner mapping yields a fact-selection question without invented ALLOW/DENY. Invalid JSON/duplicate key/unsupported schema/bad UTF-8/privileged extras -> non-success review case with structural reason, no candidate/digest fabrication/raw echo. Test missing/mismatched source/rule refs, immutable history, stale answer/version claims, unknown/type-invalid reasons, exact/+1 question/byte bounds, unsupported executable predicates, injected instructions and seeded canary absence from owned errors/repr/logs. No reply or forged APPROVED/receipt can grant execution. Guarded import/classification must deny socket/DNS/native/process/write/SDK effects. Questions are based on diagnosed missing facts, not model-invented policy winners.

## 9. Change Control and Multi-Agent Coordination

- Prefer **one source of status truth**: this root `todo.md`. Do not keep a separate hand-edited `docs/ROADMAP.md` status ledger; that file, if created, should summarize or link to the live tracker.
- `PRD.MD` defines requirements; `AGENTS.md` agent conduct; `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` WBS and DoD; ADRs approved choices; **this file records actual work evidence and current status**.
- Agent must inspect repo state and this ledger before picking a task; if work-in-progress owner/task already exists, coordinate ownership rather than edit the same files.
- In parallel work, claim explicit paths/modules and agree schema changes first. Use independent branches/PRs for conflicting areas. Merge the task tracker carefully and recompute milestone counts after resolving conflicts.
- All status and evidence changes belong in version control. Never silently delete a blocked/disputed issue, approval history or prior test failure.
- Changing WBS scope/priority/dependencies requires an update to `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`/`PRD.MD` through the documented change process first; this ledger only mirrors the authoritative approved plan.
- New unexpected bugs go into `ISS-####`; newly approved feature work must first appear in architecture WBS (or an explicitly approved extension section), then the tracker.
- Do not edit tracked acceptance wording to make unfinished work look finished.
- Before ending a session, publish a handoff: task, status, diff, tests, security implications, blockers and next task.

### Example status update (illustration only — not completed work)

```markdown
| `M1-07` | Scope guard and request-budget policy | M0-04/M1-05 | `IN_REVIEW` | @security-agent | Out-of-scope redirect blocked before outbound request | `EV-0021`; tests/security/test_scope.py; PR #42 |
```

This example is **hypothetical**, not a record of executed work.
